                      @Button@183 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Open Book #7 • 25 questions  •  New"

Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)
                        .clip_contents = true

                      @Button@207 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Open Book #11 • 25 questions  •  New"
                        .mouse_filter = 1

                      @Button@224 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Finals #1 • 70 questions  •  New"
                        .mouse_filter = 1
                        .theme_override_styles/normal = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=12.0, content_margin_top=8.0, content_margin_right=12.0, content_margin_bottom=8.0, bg_color=Color(0.043137256, 0.07058824, 0.12941177, 1), border_width_left=1, border_width_top=3, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.20392157, 0.827451, 0.6, 0.4), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0, 0, 0, 0.3), shadow_size=4, shadow_offset=Vector2(0, 2)}

                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)

                      @Button@248 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Finals #4 • 70 questions  •  New"

                      @Button@265 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 80), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 80)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "State Law #3 • 4 questions  •  New"

                      @Button@282 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 72), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 72)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Quick warm-up drill • 30 minutes timed"

                      @Button@290 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 72), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 72)

                      @Button@306 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 72), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 72)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Half-length diagnostic test • 120 minutes timed"
                        .mouse_filter = 1

behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 72), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}

Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off.                       @Button@334 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 72), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 72)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Wiring and Protection • 20 of 80 on the exam  •  New"
                        .mouse_filter = 1
                        .theme_override_styles/normal = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=12.0, content_margin_top=8.0, content_margin_right=12.0, content_margin_bottom=8.0, bg_color=Color(0.043137256, 0.07058824, 0.12941177, 1), border_width_left=1, border_width_top=3, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.49019608, 0.827451, 0.9882353, 0.4), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0, 0, 0, 0.3), shadow_size=4, shadow_offset=Vector2(0, 2)}

Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 72), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}

Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off.                       @Button@358 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 72), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 72)
                        .clip_contents = true
                        .size_flags_horizontal = 3
                        .tooltip_text = "Special Occupancies • 10 of 80 on the exam  •  New"
                        .mouse_filter = 1
                        .theme_override_styles/normal = StyleBoxFlat{content_margin_left=12.0, content_margin_top=8.0, content_margin_right=12.0, content_margin_bottom=8.0, bg_color=Color(0.043137256, 0.07058824, 0.12941177, 1), border_width_left=1, border_width_top=3, border_width_right=1, border_width_bottom=1, border_color=Color(0.49019608, 0.827451, 0.9882353, 0.4), corner_radius_top_left=12, corner_radius_top_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_right=12, corner_radius_bottom_left=12, shadow_color=Color(0, 0, 0, 0.3), shadow_size=4, shadow_offset=Vector2(0, 2)}

                      @Button@374 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 72), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
                        .custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 72)

                      @Button@387 <Button> res://src/app/menu/menu_tile.gd
                        .material = ShaderMaterial{shader=Shader{code="shader_type canvas_item;\n\n// One material for every raised surface (UiFx.add_glass / UiFx.add_shine).\n// It only touches the control's own drawing (its StyleBox, and a Button's\n// label), never child controls, and every effect is off at its default.\n//\n// Glass: inside the rounded rect the fill is lifted by fill_lift at the top\n// edge, fading to the StyleBox colour at the bottom (a vertical gradient),\n// and a 1 px highlight runs just under the top border (bevel).\n//\n// Tint: the fill blends toward tint_to across the width (the primary button's\n// cyan -> deep blue bar). Pure-white fragments are the button's label and are\n// left alone, so the text keeps its contrast.\n//\n// Shine: a diagonal light sweep; shine_pos runs -0.3 -> 1.5 (tweened).\n//\n// Current: one pulse of light runs around the border; current_pos runs\n// 0 -> 1 + current_tail (tweened), below 0 it is off.\n//\n// Flow: a slow, endless light on the border (the official simulator card),\n// flow_strength 0 = off. Runs on TIME, so it costs no CPU.\nuniform vec2 rect_size = vec2(400.0, 60.0);\nuniform float corner_radius = 12.0;\nuniform vec3 fill_lift = vec3(0.0);\nuniform float bevel = 0.0;\nuniform vec4 tint_to : source_color = vec4(0.0);\nuniform float tint_amount = 0.0;\nuniform float shine_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float shine_width = 0.12;\nuniform float strength = 0.14;\nuniform float current_pos = -1.0;\nuniform float current_tail = 0.22;\nuniform float current_strength = 1.0;\nuniform vec4 current_color : source_color = vec4(0.49, 0.83, 0.99, 1.0);\nuniform float flow_strength = 0.0;\nuniform float flow_speed = 0.08;\nuniform vec4 flow_color : source_color = vec4(0.98, 0.75, 0.14, 1.0);\n\nvarying vec2 local_pos;\n\nvoid vertex() {\n	local_pos = VERTEX;\n}\n\n// 0..1 around the outline, clockwise from the top-left corner.\nfloat perimeter_t(vec2 q, vec2 b) {\n	float dx = b.x - abs(q.x);\n	float dy = b.y - abs(q.y);\n	float per = 4.0 * (b.x + b.y);\n	float s;\n	if (dy < dx) {\n		s = q.y < 0.0 ? q.x + b.x : 2.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.x - q.x);\n	} else {\n		s = q.x > 0.0 ? 2.0 * b.x + (q.y + b.y) : 4.0 * b.x + 2.0 * b.y + (b.y - q.y);\n	}\n	return s / per;\n}\n\n// Brightness of a light head at `pos` (0..1 around the outline) with a tail.\nfloat head_at(float pos, float t, float tail) {\n	float behind = pos - t;\n	behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n	return behind <= tail ? 1.0 - behind / tail : 0.0;\n}\n\nvoid fragment() {\n	vec4 base = COLOR * texture(TEXTURE, UV);\n	vec2 size = max(rect_size, vec2(1.0));\n	vec2 p = local_pos / size;\n	vec2 b = size * 0.5;\n	vec2 q = local_pos - b;\n	vec2 k = abs(q) - b + vec2(corner_radius);\n	float sdf = length(max(k, vec2(0.0))) + min(max(k.x, k.y), 0.0) - corner_radius;\n	bool is_label = min(base.r, min(base.g, base.b)) > 0.97;\n	if (sdf <= 0.0 && !is_label) {\n		base.rgb += fill_lift * (1.0 - clamp(p.y, 0.0, 1.0));\n		base.rgb = mix(base.rgb, tint_to.rgb, tint_amount * clamp(p.x, 0.0, 1.0));\n		if (bevel > 0.0) {\n			float band = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, abs(local_pos.y - 1.5));\n			float ends = smoothstep(0.0, corner_radius, min(local_pos.x, size.x - local_pos.x));\n			base.rgb += vec3(bevel * band * ends);\n		}\n	}\n	float d = abs((p.x + p.y * 0.35) - shine_pos);\n	base.rgb += vec3((1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, shine_width, d)) * strength);\n	float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 4.0, abs(sdf + 1.0));\n	if (current_pos >= 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float behind = current_pos - along;\n		behind = behind < 0.0 ? behind + 1.0 : behind;\n		float head = behind <= current_tail && current_pos - behind >= -0.001 ? 1.0 - behind / current_tail : 0.0;\n		float glow = head * head * edge * current_strength;\n		base.rgb += current_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.85);\n	}\n	if (flow_strength > 0.0) {\n		float along = perimeter_t(q, b);\n		float t = fract(TIME * flow_speed);\n		float h = head_at(t, along, 0.18) + head_at(fract(t + 0.5), along, 0.18);\n		float glow = h * h * edge * flow_strength;\n		base.rgb += flow_color.rgb * glow;\n		base.a = max(base.a, glow * 0.7);\n	}\n	COLOR = base;\n}\n"}, shader_parameter/rect_size=Vector2(24, 96), shader_parameter/corner_radius=12.0, shader_parameter/fill_lift=Vector3(0.03529412, 0.04705882, 0.07058823), shader_parameter/bevel=0.07, shader_parameter/tint_to=null, shader_parameter/tint_amount=null, shader_parameter/shine_pos=null, shader_parameter/shine_width=null, shader_parameter/strength=null, shader_parameter/current_pos=null, shader_parameter/current_tail=null, shader_parameter/current_strength=null, shader_parameter/current_color=null, shader_parameter/flow_strength=null, shader_parameter/flow_speed=null, shader_parameter/flow_color=null}
